While fly logs --tail is running, open the install/callback URL again so the error prints.

2) Confirm which route is failing (install vs callback)
If you’re seeing the 500 when you visit:

/api/shopify/install?... → the error is in install handler

If you’re seeing the 500 after Shopify redirects back to:

/api/shopify/callback?... → the error is in callback handler (HMAC/state/token exchange)

If you paste the URL path that’s returning 500, I can pinpoint faster.

3) Verify the Shopify env vars inside the running machine
Your earlier SSH commands failed because Fly runs the command directly (no pipe parsing) unless you run a shell.

Common cause of 500 on install/callback is one of these being missing or malformed:

SHOPIFY_API_KEY

SHOPIFY_API_SECRET

SHOPIFY_APP_URL (must be https://ones4app.fly.dev — no trailing slash is safest)

COOKIE_SECRET (must be set if you’re using signed cookies/state)

4) Two very common 500 causes in your situation
A) Your app URL / redirect URL mismatch
Shopify will hard-fail OAuth if:

Shopify app “App URL” and “Allowed redirection URL(s)” don’t match exactly what your app uses.

You generally want:

App URL: https://ones4app.fly.dev

Allowed redirect URL: https://ones4app.fly.dev/api/shopify/callback

If your code builds redirect URLs using PAYLOAD_DOMAIN or PAYLOAD_PUBLIC_SERVER_URL and those don’t equal the Fly domain, you can get bad redirects/state.

B) State cookie not being set or not returned
If you’re seeing “Invalid state” sometimes and 500 other times:

the cookie may not be set due to missing cookie-parser, missing COOKIE_SECRET, wrong secure/sameSite flags, or your app being embedded vs top-level redirect.

A 500 can happen if your code assumes state exists and does something like state.length or tries to decrypt with an undefined secret.

5) One key clarification: Fly “secrets list” is supposed to look like that
Fly shows DIGESTS, not real values. They are hashes so you can tell “changed vs not changed.” Fly is not overwriting your values; it’s just not displaying them.

ChatGPT said:
Good — this log is definitive.

You are calling res.cookie(..., { signed: true }) in your Shopify install route, but your Express app does not have cookie-parser configured with a secret. That’s why the install endpoint blows up and you get “Invalid state” / “Internal Server Error”.

Fix (required): add cookie-parser with a secret before your routes
1) Install cookie-parser
From your project root:

yarn add cookie-parser
If TypeScript complains locally, also add types:

yarn add -D @types/cookie-parser
2) Add middleware in your server entry file (the one that creates app)
In the file where you have:

const app = express();
app.use(cors());
Add:

import cookieParser from "cookie-parser";

const cookieSecret = process.env.COOKIE_SECRET || process.env.PAYLOAD_SECRET || "dev-cookie-secret";

app.use(cookieParser(cookieSecret));
Important: This must be placed before you mount your Shopify routes (e.g., app.use('/api/shopify', shopifyRouter)).

So the order should be:

const app = express();

app.use(cors());
app.use(cookieParser(cookieSecret));

// THEN your routes
app.use("/api/shopify", shopifyRouter);
3) Make sure Fly has COOKIE_SECRET set
You already have it in fly secrets list (COOKIE_SECRET ...), so you’re fine.

If you ever need to set it again:

fly secrets set COOKIE_SECRET="some-long-random-string" -a ones4app
Why this fixes “Invalid state”
Your OAuth flow typically does:

/install: generate state, store it in a signed cookie

redirect to Shopify

/callback: read the signed cookie and compare to state query param

Without cookieParser(secret), Express cannot set/read signed cookies, so the state check fails.

After you apply the change
Yes — you do need to deploy because this is server code running on Fly:

fly deploy -a ones4app
Then hit:

https://ones4app.fly.dev/api/shopify/install?shop=ones4-2.myshopify.com
If you still see an error after that, paste the next log stack trace and we’ll fix the next blocker (likely HMAC verification or token exchange).

ChatGPT said:
Those warnings are common in mixed Node/Yarn repos and (importantly) none of them block cookie-parser from installing—your install succeeded.

Here is what each warning means, and what you should do (priority order).

1) package-lock.json found (important to clean up)
You have both:

yarn.lock (Yarn)

package-lock.json (npm)

This can absolutely cause “works locally / fails on Fly” because dependency resolution can diverge.

What to do

If you are using Yarn (you are): delete package-lock.json and commit that deletion.

Windows PowerShell:

del package-lock.json
Keep yarn.lock as the single source of truth.

That will remove the warning and reduce deploy surprises.

2) Resolution field "jackspeak..." incompatible (low risk)
This comes from a Yarn “resolutions” override somewhere in your dependency tree. It usually does not break builds unless the overridden version is truly incompatible.

Action

Ignore unless you start seeing runtime errors related to that package.

3) bare-fs / bare-os invalid engine (low risk)
Some packages declare an engine field that Yarn doesn’t recognize (bare). It’s noisy but not fatal.

Action

Ignore.

4) “unmet peer dependency …” (usually low risk)
Peer deps are warnings saying: “this package expects you to also have X installed”.

What matters here:
Payload admin uses React internally, but you don’t necessarily have React in your root dependency tree, so Yarn warns. Payload bundles its admin; peer deps can still warn.

Webpack peer deps warnings are common when tooling pulls Webpack indirectly.

Action

Do not start adding random peer deps unless the build fails with “Cannot find module …” or a webpack error.

If your build/deploy is currently working aside from Shopify routes, leave these alone.

5) “Workspaces can only be enabled in private projects.”
That means your package.json has "workspaces": [...] but the package is not marked private.

Action (optional)
If this is your own app (not meant to be published to npm), set:

"private": true
in the root package.json.
This silences the warning and is best practice for monorepos.

ChatGPT said:
That error is purely TypeScript order-of-statements:

TS2448: Block-scoped variable 'app' used before its declaration means you are calling something like app.use(...) (likely cookieParser(...)) before you do const app = express().

Fix (server.ts): declare app first, then app.use(...)
Use this exact ordering:

import * as dotenv from "dotenv";
dotenv.config();

import express from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import cookieParser from "cookie-parser";
import payload from "payload";
import path from "path";

const app = express();                 // ✅ 1) create app first
app.use(cors());

const cookieSecret =
  process.env.COOKIE_SECRET || process.env.PAYLOAD_SECRET || "dev-cookie-secret";

app.get("/", (req, res) => {
  res.sendFile(path.join(appDistPath, "index.html"));
});

const start = async () => {
  await payload.init({
    secret: process.env.PAYLOAD_SECRET || "dev-secret",
    express: app,
    onInit: async () => {
      payload.logger.info(`Payload Admin URL: ${payload.getAdminURL()}`);
    },
    email: undefined as any,
  });

  const port = Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080;
  app.listen(port, "0.0.0.0", () => {
    payload.logger.info(`Server listening on http://localhost:${port}`);
  });
};

start().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});

ChatGPT said:
Yes—your server.ts is currently out of order and has duplicate/conflicting middleware. That is exactly why you got TS2448 and it will also keep breaking your Shopify state cookie flow.

The two critical problems
You call app.use(...) before const app = express():

app.use(cookieParser(process.env.COOKIE_SECRET));
const app = express();
That must be reversed.

You are registering cookieParser() three different times, and you also mix JSON body parsing with express.raw() in the wrong places. Shopify webhooks must be express.raw() and must be mounted before express.json().

Replace your file structure with this clean version
This keeps only what you need, in the correct order, and avoids duplicates.

import * as dotenv from "dotenv";
dotenv.config();

import express from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import cookieParser from "cookie-parser";
import payload from "payload";
import path from "path";

import shopifyRouter from "./routes/shopify";
import shopifyWebhooksRouter from "./routes/shopify-webhooks";

const app = express();

// Fly / reverse proxy
app.set("trust proxy", 1);

// CORS
app.use(cors());

// Cookies (SIGNED)
const cookieSecret =
  process.env.COOKIE_SECRET || process.env.PAYLOAD_SECRET || "dev-cookie-secret";
app.use(cookieParser(cookieSecret));

// IMPORTANT: Shopify webhooks must be RAW body (before express.json)
app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks", express.raw({ type: "application/json" }));
app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks", shopifyWebhooksRouter);

// Normal JSON for the rest of the API
app.use(express.json());

// Shopify OAuth routes
app.use("/api/shopify", shopifyRouter);

// Serve built frontend (if present)
const appDistPath = path.join(__dirname, "app/dist");
app.use("/", express.static(appDistPath));
app.get("/", (req, res) => {
  res.sendFile(path.join(appDistPath, "index.html"));
});

const start = async () => {
  await payload.init({
    secret: process.env.PAYLOAD_SECRET || "dev-secret",
    express: app,
    onInit: async () => {
      payload.logger.info(`Payload Admin URL: ${payload.getAdminURL()}`);
    },
    email: undefined as any,
  });

  const port = Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080;
  app.listen(port, "0.0.0.0", () => {
    payload.logger.info(`Server listening on 0.0.0.0:${port}`);
  });
};

start().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});
What to delete from your current file
Remove all of these (they are redundant / harmful):

app.use(cookieParser(process.env.COOKIE_SECRET)); (before app exists)

app.use(cookieParser()); (unsigned cookies breaks res.cookie({ signed:true }))

any second/third app.set("trust proxy", 1)

shopifyRoutes vs shopifyRouter duplication (keep only one import + mount)

any webhook app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks"... inside start() (mount it once, outside)

After you change it
Run locally:

yarn build:server
Then deploy:

fly deploy -a ones4app
If you still get “Invalid state” after this, it will be because the /install route is not setting a signed cookie (or you’re using different cookie names/secrets between install and callback). Paste your routes/shopify.ts install + callback handlers and I’ll tell you exactly what to adjust.

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 17s
That webhook error is coming from Shopify’s “protected customer data” controls.

Why you’re getting this
orders/create payloads can contain customer-related information (orders are explicitly classified as protected customer data). Shopify will block creating order webhooks unless your app is allowed to access protected customer data. 
Shopify

So your code is fine; Shopify is refusing the webhook registration at the platform level.

What to do now (two valid paths)
Option A (recommended for now): Keep using the dev store, but enable protected customer data access in Partner Dashboard
For apps installed on development stores, Shopify lets you access protected customer data in development after you select the data/fields you use in the Partner Dashboard—you don’t need to submit for review just to test on dev stores. 
Shopify

Steps:

Open Partner Dashboard → Apps → your app

Go to API access requests

Find Protected customer data access → Request access

Select Protected customer data (and any fields you truly need), add justification, Save 
Shopify

Re-run your install flow and webhook registration after you’ve saved those settings.

If your app needs customer-identifying fields (email, address, phone, name), those are requested separately. 
Shopify

Option B: Use a real store + custom app (later)
A custom app installed on a specific store often has broader access compared to a public app going through App Store review gates. But for your current stage, a dev store is the correct choice—you only move to a real store when you’re ready to validate production behavior and compliance.

Immediate workaround so you can keep moving today
Register only non-protected topics first, and add order webhooks after access is enabled:

products/update (generally not customer data)

app/uninstalled

(Optionally) shop/update

Then keep your “orders sync” logic behind a feature flag until Shopify grants/recognizes protected access.

Quick check: you may also need scopes
Even with protected customer access, Shopify still expects correct scopes. For orders webhooks you typically need at least:

read_orders

So ensure your SHOPIFY_SCOPES includes the right scopes and that you reinstall the app after changing scopes (Shopify only applies scope changes on reinstall).

Answer to your earlier question
Use the dev store for now. It’s the standard approach while building OAuth, token storage, and webhook processing. Move to a real store only when you’re ready for production validation and data compliance.

If you paste the section of your webhook-registration code (the function that calls Shopify to create the webhook), I’ll tell you exactly which topics to keep enabled now, and how to structure “orders webhooks” so they automatically turn on once protected customer data access is available.

ChatGPT said:
Great question — this part is critical and easy to get wrong. I’ll explain it cleanly and practically.

What “SHOPIFY_SCOPES must match” actually means
You configure scopes in two places, and they must be identical:

Shopify App → Version → Scopes (UI)

Fly environment variable → SHOPIFY_SCOPES

If they don’t match:

OAuth may succeed but permissions will be wrong

Webhook creation may fail

Shopify may reject installs silently or with vague errors

Step-by-step: do it correctly
1️⃣ Decide the scopes FIRST (single source of truth)
For where you are now, I recommend starting with safe, minimal scopes:

read_products,read_orders
(We can add more later if needed.)

2️⃣ Set scopes in Shopify (new version)
In Shopify Dev Dashboard → App → Versions → New Version:

Find Access → Scopes

Enter exactly (no spaces at start/end):

read_products,read_orders
✔ Save
✔ Release the version

3️⃣ Set the SAME scopes in Fly secrets
In your local terminal (PowerShell is fine):

fly secrets set SHOPIFY_SCOPES="read_products,read_orders" -a ones4app
Important rules:

Comma-separated

No spaces unless you include them everywhere

Case-sensitive

Must match Shopify exactly

4️⃣ Restart (deploy or restart machines)
You do not need a full rebuild if code didn’t change, but one of these must happen:

Option A (simple):

fly deploy
Option B (faster):

fly machines restart -a ones4app
5️⃣ Reinstall the app (mandatory after scope change)
Scopes do not apply until reinstall.

Open in browser:

https://ones4app.fly.dev/api/shopify/install?shop=ones4-2.myshopify.com
You should see:

Shopify permission screen

The scopes you entered

Approval → redirect to /api/shopify/callback

fly secrets list -a ones4app
You won’t see the value, but confirm:

SHOPIFY_SCOPES
exists (only once).

In your install route (temporarily), log:

console.log("Using scopes:", process.env.SHOPIFY_SCOPES)
You should see:

read_products,read_orders
Remove this log after confirming.

Common mistakes (avoid these)
❌ Shopify UI: read_products, read_orders
❌ Fly secret: read_products,read_orders
→ These do NOT match (extra space)

❌ Changing scopes but not reinstalling
→ Old token still in effect

❌ Updating Shopify but forgetting Fly
→ OAuth mismatch

When you add more scopes later
Example: adding customers (requires compliance)

Update Shopify version scopes

Update Fly secret

Release

Reinstall app

Always in that order.

import { Router } from "express";
import crypto from "crypto";
import payload from "payload";

const router = Router();

const SHOPIFY_API_VERSION = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_VERSION || "2025-10";
const SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG = "shops"; // <- change if your Payload collection slug differs

function timingSafeEqualStr(a: string, b: string) {
  const aBuf = Buffer.from(a, "utf8");
  const bBuf = Buffer.from(b, "utf8");
  if (aBuf.length !== bBuf.length) return false;
  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(aBuf, bBuf);
}

/**
 * HMAC verification for Shopify OAuth callback querystring
 * Shopify computes HMAC over sorted query params (excluding hmac and signature)
 */
function verifyOAuthHmac(query: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>, secret: string) {
  const hmac = String(query.hmac || "");
  if (!hmac) return false;

  const message = Object.keys(query)
    .filter((k) => k !== "hmac" && k !== "signature")
    .sort()
    .map((k) => {
      const v = query[k];
      if (Array.isArray(v)) return ${k}=${v.join(",")};
      return ${k}=${String(v ?? "")};
    })
    .join("&");

  const digest = crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(message).digest("hex");
  return timingSafeEqualStr(digest, hmac);
}

async function registerWebhook(params: {
  shop: string;
  accessToken: string;
  topic: string;
  address: string;
}) {
  const { shop, accessToken, topic, address } = params;

  const res = await fetch(https://${shop}/admin/api/${SHOPIFY_API_VERSION}/webhooks.json, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "X-Shopify-Access-Token": accessToken,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      webhook: {
        topic,
        address,
        format: "json",
      },
    }),
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    throw new Error(Webhook create failed (${topic}): ${text});
  }

  return res.json();
}

/**
 * STEP 3 — START OAUTH (INSTALL)
 * GET /api/shopify/install?shop=xxx.myshopify.com
 */
router.get("/install", (req, res) => {
  const shop = String(req.query.shop || "").trim();

  if (!shop || !shop.endsWith(".myshopify.com")) {
    return res.status(400).send("Invalid shop");
  }

  const apiKey = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_KEY!;
  const appUrl = process.env.SHOPIFY_APP_URL!;
  const scopes = process.env.SHOPIFY_SCOPES!;

  if (!apiKey || !appUrl || !scopes) {
    return res.status(500).send("Missing Shopify env vars");
  }

  // CSRF state
  const state = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString("hex");

  res.cookie("shopify_oauth_state", state, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: true,
    sameSite: "lax",
    signed: true,
    maxAge: 10 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  const redirectUri = ${appUrl}/api/shopify/callback;

  const installUrl =
    https://${shop}/admin/oauth/authorize +
    ?client_id=${encodeURIComponent(apiKey)} +
    &scope=${encodeURIComponent(scopes)} +
    &redirect_uri=${encodeURIComponent(redirectUri)} +
    &state=${encodeURIComponent(state)};

  return res.redirect(installUrl);
});

/**
 * STEP 4 — FINISH OAUTH (CALLBACK)
 * GET /api/shopify/callback?shop=...&code=...&state=...&hmac=...&timestamp=...
 */
router.get("/callback", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const shop = String(req.query.shop || "");
    const code = String(req.query.code || "");
    const state = String(req.query.state || "");

    if (!shop || !code || !state) {
      return res.status(400).send("Missing params");
    }

    // 1) Verify cookie state
    const savedState = req.signedCookies?.shopify_oauth_state;
    if (!savedState || savedState !== state) {
      return res.status(400).send("Invalid state");
    }
    res.clearCookie("shopify_oauth_state");

    // 2) Verify Shopify HMAC
    const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET!;
    if (!secret) return res.status(500).send("Missing SHOPIFY_API_SECRET");

    const okHmac = verifyOAuthHmac(req.query as any, secret);
    if (!okHmac) {
      return res.status(400).send("Invalid HMAC");
    }

    // 3) Exchange code → access token
    const tokenRes = await fetch(https://${shop}/admin/oauth/access_token, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        client_id: process.env.SHOPIFY_API_KEY,
        client_secret: process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET,
        code,
      }),
    });

    if (!tokenRes.ok) {
      const text = await tokenRes.text();
      return res.status(500).send(text);
    }

    const tokenJson = await tokenRes.json();
    const accessToken = tokenJson.access_token as string;
    if (!accessToken) return res.status(500).send("Missing access token from Shopify");

    // 4) Upsert into Payload: shops collection
    // Fields assumed: shopDomain, accessToken, scopes, installedAt
    const scopes = process.env.SHOPIFY_SCOPES || "";
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const existing = await payload.find({
      collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
      where: {
        shopDomain: { equals: shop },
      },
      limit: 1,
    });

    if (existing.docs?.length) {
      await payload.update({
        collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
        id: existing.docs[0].id,
        data: {
          shopDomain: shop,
          accessToken, // ideally encrypt at rest (can do later)
          scopes,
          installedAt: now,
          uninstalledAt: null,
        },
      });
    } else {
      await payload.create({
        collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
        data: {
          shopDomain: shop,
          accessToken,
          scopes,
          installedAt: now,
          uninstalledAt: null,
        },
      });
    }

    // 5) Register webhooks automatically
    const appUrl = process.env.SHOPIFY_APP_URL!;
    const webhookBase = ${appUrl}/api/shopify/webhooks;

    // Use distinct endpoints per topic (recommended)
    const webhooksToCreate = [
      { topic: "orders/create", address: ${webhookBase}/orders-create },
      { topic: "orders/updated", address: ${webhookBase}/orders-updated },
      { topic: "products/update", address: ${webhookBase}/products-update },
    ];

    for (const w of webhooksToCreate) {
      await registerWebhook({
        shop,
        accessToken,
        topic: w.topic,
        address: w.address,
      });
    }

    // Final redirect (to admin or UI)
    return res.redirect("/admin");
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error(err);
    return res.status(500).send(err?.message || "Callback failed");
  }
});

export default router;

Your install route uses signed cookies:

So you must have cookie-parser configured with a secret before this router runs, and you must clear the signed cookie correctly.

In your main server file, make sure this happens before app.use("/api/shopify", shopifyRoutes):

ChatGPT said:
You are very close, but your file has a few hard blockers (duplicate imports, using app before it exists, and defining cookieSecret twice). Below is a clean, correct, deployable src/server.ts you can paste in as the whole file.

What was wrong in yours (brief, specific)
You have import cookieParser twice.

You define cookieSecret twice.

You call app.use(cookieParser(...)) before const app = express(); (that causes the TS error and runtime issues).

Webhook raw body middleware must be applied only to webhook routes and before express.json() (you already intended this, we’ll keep it correct).

Replace your entire src/server.ts with this
import * as dotenv from "dotenv";
dotenv.config();

import express from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import cookieParser from "cookie-parser";
import payload from "payload";
import path from "path";

import shopifyRouter from "./routes/shopify";
import shopifyWebhooksRouter from "./routes/shopify-webhooks";

const app = express();

// Fly / reverse proxy (required for secure cookies + correct protocol)
app.set("trust proxy", 1);

// CORS
app.use(cors());

// Cookies (SIGNED) — must be BEFORE /api/shopify routes
const cookieSecret =
  process.env.COOKIE_SECRET ||
  process.env.PAYLOAD_SECRET ||
  "dev-cookie-secret";

app.use(cookieParser(cookieSecret));

// IMPORTANT: Shopify webhooks must receive RAW body for HMAC verification
// This must be BEFORE express.json(), and only applied to webhook routes.
app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks", express.raw({ type: "application/json" }));
app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks", shopifyWebhooksRouter);

// Normal JSON parsing for everything else
app.use(express.json());

// Shopify OAuth routes
app.use("/api/shopify", shopifyRouter);

// Serve built frontend (if present)
const appDistPath = path.join(__dirname, "app/dist");
app.use("/", express.static(appDistPath));
app.get("/", (req, res) => {
  res.sendFile(path.join(appDistPath, "index.html"));
});

const start = async () => {
  await payload.init({
    secret: process.env.PAYLOAD_SECRET || "dev-secret",
    express: app,
    onInit: async () => {
      payload.logger.info(`Payload Admin URL: ${payload.getAdminURL()}`);
    },
    // Disable email for now (prevents SMTP attempts)
    email: undefined as any,
  });

  const port = Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080;
  app.listen(port, "0.0.0.0", () => {
    payload.logger.info(`Server listening on 0.0.0.0:${port}`);
  });
};

start().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});

async function registerWebhook(params: {
  shop: string;
  accessToken: string;
  topic: string;
  address: string;
}) {
  const { shop, accessToken, topic, address } = params;

  const res = await fetch(https://${shop}/admin/api/${SHOPIFY_API_VERSION}/webhooks.json, {

router.get("/install", (req, res) => {
  const shop = String(req.query.shop || "").trim();

  if (!shop || !shop.endsWith(".myshopify.com")) {
    return res.status(400).send("Invalid shop");
  }

  const installUrl =
    https://${shop}/admin/oauth/authorize +
    ?client_id=${encodeURIComponent(apiKey)} +
    &scope=${encodeURIComponent(scopes)} +
    &redirect_uri=${encodeURIComponent(redirectUri)} +
    &state=${encodeURIComponent(state)};

  return res.redirect(installUrl);
});

/**
 * STEP 4 — FINISH OAUTH (CALLBACK)
 * GET /api/shopify/callback?shop=...&code=...&state=...&hmac=...&timestamp=...
 */
router.get("/callback", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const shop = String(req.query.shop || "");
    const code = String(req.query.code || "");
    const state = String(req.query.state || "");

    if (!shop || !code || !state) {
      return res.status(400).send("Missing params");
    }

    // 1) Verify cookie state
    const savedState = req.signedCookies?.shopify_oauth_state;
    if (!savedState || savedState !== state) {
      return res.status(400).send("Invalid state");
    }
    res.clearCookie("shopify_oauth_state");

    // 2) Verify Shopify HMAC
    const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET!;
    if (!secret) return res.status(500).send("Missing SHOPIFY_API_SECRET");

    const okHmac = verifyOAuthHmac(req.query as any, secret);
    if (!okHmac) {
      return res.status(400).send("Invalid HMAC");
    }

    // 3) Exchange code → access token
    const tokenRes = await fetch(https://${shop}/admin/oauth/access_token, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        client_id: process.env.SHOPIFY_API_KEY,
        client_secret: process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET,
        code,
      }),
    });

    if (!tokenRes.ok) {
      const text = await tokenRes.text();
      return res.status(500).send(text);
    }

    const tokenJson = await tokenRes.json();
    const accessToken = tokenJson.access_token as string;
    if (!accessToken) return res.status(500).send("Missing access token from Shopify");

    // 4) Upsert into Payload: shops collection
    // Fields assumed: shopDomain, accessToken, scopes, installedAt
    const scopes = process.env.SHOPIFY_SCOPES || "";
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const existing = await payload.find({
      collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
      where: {
        shopDomain: { equals: shop },
      },
      limit: 1,
    });

    if (existing.docs?.length) {
      await payload.update({
        collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
        id: existing.docs[0].id,
        data: {
          shopDomain: shop,
          accessToken, // ideally encrypt at rest (can do later)
          scopes,
          installedAt: now,
          uninstalledAt: null,
        },
      });
    } else {
      await payload.create({
        collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
        data: {
          shopDomain: shop,
          accessToken,
          scopes,
          installedAt: now,
          uninstalledAt: null,
        },
      });
    }

    // 5) Register webhooks automatically
    const appUrl = process.env.SHOPIFY_APP_URL!;
    const webhookBase = ${appUrl}/api/shopify/webhooks;

    // Use distinct endpoints per topic (recommended)
  const webhooksToCreate = [
  { topic: "products/update", address: ${webhookBase}/products-update },
  { topic: "app/uninstalled", address: ${webhookBase}/app-uninstalled },
];

    for (const w of webhooksToCreate) {
      await registerWebhook({
        shop,
        accessToken,
        topic: w.topic,
        address: w.address,
      });
    }

    // Final redirect (to admin or UI)
    return res.redirect("/admin");
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error(err);
    return res.status(500).send(err?.message || "Callback failed");
  }
});

export default router;
ChatGPT said:
This routes/shopify.ts is mostly correct, but there are 4 changes you should make to stop the “Invalid state” loop reliably on Fly and to make webhook registration more robust.

Below are the exact, specific edits to apply.

1) Fix cookie settings for Fly (this is the #1 cause of “Invalid state”)
Change your /install cookie options to include path and correct secure behind proxy
Replace your cookie block with this:

res.cookie("shopify_oauth_state", state, {
  httpOnly: true,
  secure: true,          // OK because you set app.set("trust proxy", 1) in server.ts
  sameSite: "lax",
  signed: true,
  path: "/",             // IMPORTANT
  maxAge: 10 * 60 * 1000,
});
Also change clearCookie to match (path + signed):

res.clearCookie("shopify_oauth_state", { path: "/" });
Why: if the cookie is set without matching clear options / path, or not stored consistently, Shopify returns with state, but your cookie is missing → “Invalid state”.

2) Ensure SHOPIFY_APP_URL has https and no trailing slash
Right now you use:

const appUrl = process.env.SHOPIFY_APP_URL!;
On Fly you often store domain only. Normalize it once:

const rawAppUrl = process.env.SHOPIFY_APP_URL!;
if (!rawAppUrl) return res.status(500).send("Missing SHOPIFY_APP_URL");

const appUrl = rawAppUrl.startsWith("http")
  ? rawAppUrl.replace(/\/$/, "")
  : `https://${rawAppUrl.replace(/\/$/, "")}`;
Then use appUrl to build redirectUri/webhookBase.

This prevents bad redirect URIs like 42c4f.../api/... or double slashes.

3) Webhook registration: don’t hard-fail the whole callback if one webhook fails
Right now registerWebhook() throws and your callback returns 500. That can break install even though token was stored.

Change:

for (const w of webhooksToCreate) {
  await registerWebhook(...);
}
to:

for (const w of webhooksToCreate) {
  try {
    await registerWebhook({
      shop,
      accessToken,
      topic: w.topic,
      address: w.address,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    // Log and continue. Do NOT fail install.
    console.error(e?.message || e);
  }
}
This is important because Shopify can reject some topics depending on configuration.

4) Add basic validation for shop and make HMAC check include redirect-specific behavior
Keep your HMAC verification as-is, but validate shop more strictly in callback too:

if (!shop.endsWith(".myshopify.com")) {
  return res.status(400).send("Invalid shop");
}
Also: your HMAC function is fine; just ensure req.query is passed as-is (you do).

  process.env.COOKIE_SECRET ||

2) src/routes/shopify.ts (REPLACE THE ENTIRE FILE)
import { Router } from "express";
import crypto from "crypto";
import payload from "payload";

const router = Router();

const SHOPIFY_API_VERSION = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_VERSION || "2025-10";
const SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG = "shops"; // must match your Payload collection slug

function timingSafeEqualStr(a: string, b: string) {
  const aBuf = Buffer.from(a, "utf8");
  const bBuf = Buffer.from(b, "utf8");
  if (aBuf.length !== bBuf.length) return false;
  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(aBuf, bBuf);
}

/**
 * Shopify OAuth callback HMAC verification
 * Build message from sorted query params excluding hmac/signature.
 */
function verifyOAuthHmac(
  query: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>,
  secret: string
) {
  const hmac = String(query.hmac || "");
  if (!hmac) return false;

  const message = Object.keys(query)
    .filter((k) => k !== "hmac" && k !== "signature")
    .sort()
    .map((k) => {
      const v = query[k];
      if (Array.isArray(v)) return `${k}=${v.join(",")}`;
      return `${k}=${String(v ?? "")}`;
    })
    .join("&");

  const digest = crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(message).digest("hex");
  return timingSafeEqualStr(digest, hmac);
}

async function registerWebhook(params: {
  shop: string;
  accessToken: string;
  topic: string;
  address: string;
}) {
  const { shop, accessToken, topic, address } = params;

  const res = await fetch(
    `https://${shop}/admin/api/${SHOPIFY_API_VERSION}/webhooks.json`,
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "X-Shopify-Access-Token": accessToken,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        webhook: { topic, address, format: "json" },
      }),
    }
  );

  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`Webhook create failed (${topic}): ${text}`);
  }

  return res.json();
}

function normalizeAppUrl(raw: string) {
  const trimmed = raw.replace(/\/$/, "");
  if (trimmed.startsWith("http://") || trimmed.startsWith("https://")) return trimmed;
  return `https://${trimmed}`;
}

/**
 * STEP 3 — START OAUTH (INSTALL)
 * GET /api/shopify/install?shop=xxx.myshopify.com
 */
router.get("/install", (req, res) => {
  const shop = String(req.query.shop || "").trim();

  if (!shop || !shop.endsWith(".myshopify.com")) {
    return res.status(400).send("Invalid shop");
  }

  const apiKey = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_KEY;
  const apiSecret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET;
  const rawAppUrl = process.env.SHOPIFY_APP_URL;
  const scopes = process.env.SHOPIFY_SCOPES;

  if (!apiKey || !apiSecret || !rawAppUrl || !scopes) {
    return res.status(500).send("Missing Shopify env vars");
  }

  const appUrl = normalizeAppUrl(rawAppUrl);

  // CSRF state
  const state = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString("hex");

  // Signed cookie requires cookieParser(secret) installed in server.ts
  res.cookie("shopify_oauth_state", state, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: true,
    sameSite: "lax",
    signed: true,
    path: "/",
    maxAge: 10 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  const redirectUri = `${appUrl}/api/shopify/callback`;

  const installUrl =
    `https://${shop}/admin/oauth/authorize` +
    `?client_id=${encodeURIComponent(apiKey)}` +
    `&scope=${encodeURIComponent(scopes)}` +
    `&redirect_uri=${encodeURIComponent(redirectUri)}` +
    `&state=${encodeURIComponent(state)}`;

  return res.redirect(installUrl);
});

/**
 * STEP 4 — FINISH OAUTH (CALLBACK)
 * GET /api/shopify/callback?shop=...&code=...&state=...&hmac=...&timestamp=...
 */
router.get("/callback", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const shop = String(req.query.shop || "").trim();
    const code = String(req.query.code || "").trim();
    const state = String(req.query.state || "").trim();

    if (!shop || !code || !state) return res.status(400).send("Missing params");
    if (!shop.endsWith(".myshopify.com")) return res.status(400).send("Invalid shop");

    // 1) Verify cookie state
    const savedState = req.signedCookies?.shopify_oauth_state;
    if (!savedState || savedState !== state) {
      return res.status(400).send("Invalid state");
    }
    res.clearCookie("shopify_oauth_state", { path: "/" });

    // 2) Verify Shopify HMAC
    const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET;
    if (!secret) return res.status(500).send("Missing SHOPIFY_API_SECRET");

    const okHmac = verifyOAuthHmac(req.query as any, secret);
    if (!okHmac) return res.status(400).send("Invalid HMAC");

    // 3) Exchange code → access token
    const tokenRes = await fetch(`https://${shop}/admin/oauth/access_token`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        client_id: process.env.SHOPIFY_API_KEY,
        client_secret: process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET,
        code,
      }),
    });

    if (!tokenRes.ok) {
      const text = await tokenRes.text();
      return res.status(500).send(text);
    }

    const tokenJson = await tokenRes.json();
    const accessToken = tokenJson.access_token as string;
    if (!accessToken) return res.status(500).send("Missing access token from Shopify");

    // 4) Upsert into Payload: shops collection
    const scopes = process.env.SHOPIFY_SCOPES || "";
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const existing = await payload.find({
      collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
      where: { shopDomain: { equals: shop } },
      limit: 1,
    });

    if (existing.docs?.length) {
      await payload.update({
        collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
        id: existing.docs[0].id,
        data: {
          shopDomain: shop,
          accessToken,
          scopes,
          installedAt: now,
          uninstalledAt: null,
        },
      });
    } else {
      await payload.create({
        collection: SHOPS_COLLECTION_SLUG,
        data: {
          shopDomain: shop,
          accessToken,
          scopes,
          installedAt: now,
          uninstalledAt: null,
        },
      });
    }

    // 5) Register webhooks automatically (safe: do not fail install if one is blocked)
    const rawAppUrl = process.env.SHOPIFY_APP_URL;
    if (rawAppUrl) {
      const appUrl = normalizeAppUrl(rawAppUrl);
      const webhookBase = `${appUrl}/api/shopify/webhooks`;

      const webhooksToCreate = [
        { topic: "products/update", address: `${webhookBase}/products-update` },
        { topic: "app/uninstalled", address: `${webhookBase}/app-uninstalled` },
        // NOTE: orders/* may fail unless you have protected customer data access
      ];

      for (const w of webhooksToCreate) {
        try {
          await registerWebhook({
            shop,
            accessToken,
            topic: w.topic,
            address: w.address,
          });
        } catch (e: any) {
          console.error(e?.message || e);
        }
      }
    }

    // Final redirect (Payload admin)
    return res.redirect("/admin");
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error(err);
    return res.status(500).send(err?.message || "Callback failed");
  }
});

export default router;

Then deploy only if you changed code:

ChatGPT said:
Your webhook file is close, but it needs three fixes to work reliably with Shopify + Fly:

Use the raw body (you already mounted express.raw() in server.ts, good)

Verify HMAC correctly using the raw bytes and compare the base64 strings in a timing-safe way

Actually parse the JSON payload (after verifying) and optionally persist / mark uninstall

Also: since you currently cannot register orders/create webhooks (protected customer data), your webhook router should include handlers for the topics you can use now: products/update and app/uninstalled. You can keep orders/* handlers, but don’t rely on them until Shopify grants access.

Below is a complete “whole file” replacement for src/routes/shopify-webhooks.ts.

src/routes/shopify-webhooks.ts (REPLACE ENTIRE FILE)
import { Router } from "express";
import crypto from "crypto";
import payload from "payload";

const router = Router();

function timingSafeEqualBase64(aBase64: string, bBase64: string) {
  const a = Buffer.from(aBase64, "utf8");
  const b = Buffer.from(bBase64 || "", "utf8");
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b);
}

function verifyWebhookHmac(rawBody: Buffer, hmacHeader: string, secret: string) {
  // Shopify expects base64 of HMAC-SHA256 over RAW request body
  const digest = crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(rawBody).digest("base64");
  return timingSafeEqualBase64(digest, hmacHeader);
}

function getShopFromHeader(req: any) {
  // Shopify sends shop domain in this header on webhooks
  return String(req.headers["x-shopify-shop-domain"] || "").trim();
}

router.post("/products-update", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET;
    if (!secret) return res.status(500).send("Missing SHOPIFY_API_SECRET");

    const hmac = String(req.headers["x-shopify-hmac-sha256"] || "");
    const ok = verifyWebhookHmac(req.body as Buffer, hmac, secret);
    if (!ok) return res.status(401).send("Invalid webhook HMAC");

    const shop = getShopFromHeader(req);
    const bodyStr = (req.body as Buffer).toString("utf8");
    const data = JSON.parse(bodyStr);

    // TODO: upsert product into your Payload collection if you want.
    // Example fields might be: shopDomain, shopifyProductId, title, variants, images, updatedAt

    // Basic log for now:
    console.log("products/update", { shop, productId: data?.id });

    return res.status(200).send("ok");
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error(err);
    return res.status(500).send(err?.message || "Webhook error");
  }
});

router.post("/app-uninstalled", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET;
    if (!secret) return res.status(500).send("Missing SHOPIFY_API_SECRET");

    const hmac = String(req.headers["x-shopify-hmac-sha256"] || "");
    const ok = verifyWebhookHmac(req.body as Buffer, hmac, secret);
    if (!ok) return res.status(401).send("Invalid webhook HMAC");

    const shop = getShopFromHeader(req);

    // Mark shop uninstalled in Payload (shops collection)
    const existing = await payload.find({
      collection: "shops",
      where: { shopDomain: { equals: shop } },
      limit: 1,
    });

    if (existing.docs?.length) {
      await payload.update({
        collection: "shops",
        id: existing.docs[0].id,
        data: { uninstalledAt: new Date().toISOString() },
      });
    }

    console.log("app/uninstalled", { shop });
    return res.status(200).send("ok");
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error(err);
    return res.status(500).send(err?.message || "Webhook error");
  }
});

// Keep these handlers only if you later get protected customer data access.
// They should still verify + parse to avoid future surprises.
router.post("/orders-create", (req, res) => {
  const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET;
  if (!secret) return res.status(500).send("Missing SHOPIFY_API_SECRET");

  const hmac = String(req.headers["x-shopify-hmac-sha256"] || "");
  const ok = verifyWebhookHmac(req.body as Buffer, hmac, secret);
  if (!ok) return res.status(401).send("Invalid webhook HMAC");

  // const data = JSON.parse((req.body as Buffer).toString("utf8"));
  return res.status(200).send("ok");
});

router.post("/orders-updated", (req, res) => {
  const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET;
  if (!secret) return res.status(500).send("Missing SHOPIFY_API_SECRET");

  const hmac = String(req.headers["x-shopify-hmac-sha256"] || "");
  const ok = verifyWebhookHmac(req.body as Buffer, hmac, secret);
  if (!ok) return res.status(401).send("Invalid webhook HMAC");

  // const data = JSON.parse((req.body as Buffer).toString("utf8"));
  return res.status(200).send("ok");
});

export default router;
Two important checks (so this file actually receives RAW bytes)
A) Confirm server.ts mounts raw body BEFORE json (you should have this)
You need:

app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks", express.raw(...))

then app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks", shopifyWebhooksRouter)

then later app.use(express.json())

You already moved that earlier—keep it exactly like that.

B) Do not add express.json() inside the webhook router
If you add json parsing on the webhook route, Shopify HMAC will fail.

Your logs look healthy for Fly + Payload + Mongo. The only real problem showing is this:

Webhook create failed (orders/create) → Shopify blocked it as protected customer data.

Everything else (server on 0.0.0.0:8080, admin URL, Mongo connected) is correct.

1) Fix the webhook registration so it does NOT break install/callback
Right now, your /callback is attempting to register orders/create and throwing, which can cause your install flow to end in an error (or at least log a scary stack).

Change your webhook loop to skip protected topics for now
In src/routes/shopify.ts (your OAuth callback file), find this part:

const webhooksToCreate = [
  { topic: "orders/create", address: `${webhookBase}/orders-create` },
  { topic: "orders/updated", address: `${webhookBase}/orders-updated` },
  { topic: "products/update", address: `${webhookBase}/products-update` },
];
Replace with:

const webhooksToCreate = [
  { topic: "products/update", address: `${webhookBase}/products-update` },
  { topic: "app/uninstalled", address: `${webhookBase}/app-uninstalled` },
];
And make the registration non-fatal (so even if one fails, install still succeeds):

for (const w of webhooksToCreate) {
  try {
    await registerWebhook({
      shop,
      accessToken,
      topic: w.topic,
      address: w.address,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error(`Webhook registration failed: ${w.topic}`, e?.message || e);
    // DO NOT throw; allow install to complete
  }
}
This keeps your OAuth flow stable while Shopify blocks orders.

2) Update your webhook routes to match what you register
Your webhook router currently has:

/orders-create

/orders-updated

/products-update

If you register app/uninstalled, add this route in src/routes/shopify-webhooks.ts:

router.post("/app-uninstalled", (req, res) => {
  const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET!;
  const hmac = String(req.headers["x-shopify-hmac-sha256"] || "");
  const ok = verifyWebhookHmac(req.body as Buffer, hmac, secret);
  if (!ok) return res.status(401).send("Invalid webhook HMAC");

  // TODO: mark shop uninstalled in DB
  return res.status(200).send("ok");
});
(Keep products-update.)

These lines are normal during deploy/rolling restart:

So don’t chase those.

1) Make webhook registration non-fatal (do this in /callback)
Immediately below your webhooksToCreate array, your loop should look exactly like this:

for (const w of webhooksToCreate) {
  try {
    await registerWebhook({
      shop,
      accessToken,
      topic: w.topic,
      address: w.address,

}
That’s it for shopify.ts.

B) In src/routes/shopify-webhooks.ts (your webhooks receiver)
Replace the whole file with this exact code
import { Router } from "express";
import crypto from "crypto";
import payload from "payload";

const router = Router();

function verifyWebhookHmac(rawBody: Buffer, hmacHeader: string, secret: string) {
  const digest = crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(rawBody).digest("base64");
  const a = Buffer.from(digest, "utf8");
  const b = Buffer.from(hmacHeader || "", "utf8");
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b);
}

router.post("/products-update", (req, res) => {
  const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET!;
  const hmac = String(req.headers["x-shopify-hmac-sha256"] || "");
  const ok = verifyWebhookHmac(req.body as Buffer, hmac, secret);
  if (!ok) return res.status(401).send("Invalid webhook HMAC");

  // Optional later: const data = JSON.parse((req.body as Buffer).toString("utf8"));
  return res.status(200).send("ok");
});

router.post("/app-uninstalled", async (req, res) => {
  const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET!;
  const hmac = String(req.headers["x-shopify-hmac-sha256"] || "");
  const ok = verifyWebhookHmac(req.body as Buffer, hmac, secret);
  if (!ok) return res.status(401).send("Invalid webhook HMAC");

  const body = JSON.parse((req.body as Buffer).toString("utf8"));
  const shop = String(body?.myshopify_domain || body?.domain || "");

  if (shop) {
    // mark as uninstalled (assumes shops collection + shopDomain field)
    const existing = await payload.find({
      collection: "shops",
      where: { shopDomain: { equals: shop } },
      limit: 1,
    });

    if (existing.docs?.length) {
      await payload.update({
        collection: "shops",
        id: existing.docs[0].id,
        data: { uninstalledAt: new Date().toISOString() },
      });
    }
  }

  return res.status(200).send("ok");
});

export default router;
C) In src/server.ts (Express setup)
You must already have these two lines in this order:

1) BEFORE express.json() add raw body for webhooks:
app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks", express.raw({ type: "application/json" }));
2) Mount the webhooks router immediately after:
app.use("/api/shopify/webhooks", shopifyWebhooksRouter);
(Then later you can have app.use(express.json()) for everything else.)

D) Deploy and re-test
fly deploy
Then re-run install:

https://ones4app.fly.dev/api/shopify/install?shop=ones4-2.myshopify.com
Result you want: install completes and redirects to /admin even if webhooks fail (they should not fail now because we removed orders topics).

Step 2 — Update webhook route to actually store the product

Replace your /products-update route with this (copy/paste):

router.post("/products-update", async (req, res) => {
  const secret = process.env.SHOPIFY_API_SECRET!;
  const hmac = String(req.headers["x-shopify-hmac-sha256"] || "");
  const ok = verifyWebhookHmac(req.body as Buffer, hmac, secret);
  if (!ok) return res.status(401).send("Invalid webhook HMAC");

  const topic = String(req.headers["x-shopify-topic"] || "");
  const shop = String(req.headers["x-shopify-shop-domain"] || "");

  // Parse webhook JSON (req.body is Buffer because we used express.raw)
  const data = JSON.parse((req.body as Buffer).toString("utf8"));

  // Shopify product id is usually `id`
  const shopifyProductId = String(data.id || "");

  if (!shop || !shopifyProductId) {
    return res.status(400).send("Missing shop or product id");
  }

  // Upsert into Payload "products" collection
  const existing = await payload.find({
    collection: "products",
    where: {
      and: [
        { shopDomain: { equals: shop } },
        { shopifyProductId: { equals: shopifyProductId } },
      ],
    },
    limit: 1,
  });

  if (existing.docs?.length) {
    await payload.update({
      collection: "products",
      id: existing.docs[0].id,
      data: {
        shopDomain: shop,
        shopifyProductId,
        title: data.title || "",
        raw: data,
      },
    });
  } else {
    await payload.create({
      collection: "products",
      data: {
        shopDomain: shop,
        shopifyProductId,
        title: data.title || "",
        raw: data,
      },
    });
  }

  return res.status(200).send("ok");
});
That is the minimum working version.
